([r-uben/disputatio](https://github.com/r-uben/disputatio/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/methodology-section-writer.md) · ★1)

- **Metis Arbiter**: L4 terminal leaf - ARBITER. Independent decision-maker for forks the loop cannot resolve on its own. Under UNATTENDED mode it ALWAYS rules and continues, NEVER escalates to the user. Output is a binding ruling logged to the ledger. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-arbiter.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Coordinator**: Orchestrates complex multi-step tasks by breaking them down into disjoint lanes and delegating to specialist subagents (planner, implementer, reviewer, verifier, depth-prober, juror, goal-checker). ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-coordinator.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Depth-prober**: L4 terminal leaf - G3.5 DEPTH-LOCK. Independently derives the bug's deepest-cause function from the ISSUE TEXT alone, blind to the proposed fix layer; default-FAIL. Emits D1-D5. depth-miss REJECTs to G1. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-depth-prober.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Engineer**: Implements one explicitly assigned, ready-for-agent Metis issue while respecting repository architecture, continuous-development classes, rewrite-risk mitigation, lifecycle VSA where applicable, release controls, tests, and human review gates. ([WilliamGomes41/Metis-DS](https://github.com/WilliamGomes41/Metis-DS/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/metis-engineer.agent.md))
- **Metis Execharness-resolver**: L3 executor - EXECHARNESS RESOLVE. Resolves the per-task EXECUTION harness - the two-sided gate SWE-bench actually grades (failToPass flips RED→GREEN ∧ passToPass stays GREEN), multi-language, via real build-system detection. Ingests shipped FAIL_TO_PASS/PASS_TO_PASS, else derives failToPass from the mission's behavioral acceptance asks. An unresolvable environment is BLOCKED, never a stand-in. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-execharness-resolver.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Feature-coordinator**: L1 feature coordinator - drives approved ROADMAP.md lanes through their required build/review/verification gates and owns the run-wide feature frontier. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-feature-coordinator.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Framework-generator**: L3 executor - FRAMEWORK GENERATE. When the SELECTOR returns MISS, generates a one-off custom framework for the exact task shape - classifies the orthogonal axes, composes the gate sequence from the GATE-LIBRARY with the correct axis-specific gate, emits the gen-&lt;axis-signature&gt; leaf with the BLOCKED invariant verbatim, binds an execharness, and hands it to the validator before any gate runs. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-framework-generator.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Framework-validator**: L4 terminal leaf - FRAMEWORK VALIDATE (HRN-5). A fresh, default-FAIL juror that proves a GENERATED framework is SOUND before any gate runs. Checks the HRN-5 default-FAIL checklist - every gate mapped, exactly one terminal DONE with negatives looping UP, the BLOCKED invariant verbatim, a non-empty acceptance set. PASS lets the leaf be driven; FAIL with numbered reasons returns it to the generator. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-framework-validator.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Fresh-verifier**: L4 blind fresh verifier - independently checks a candidate roadmap or plan against the exact mission and repository; APPROVE/REJECT, default-FAIL. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-fresh-verifier.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Goal-checker**: L4 terminal leaf - GOAL-CHECK. Independent, adversarial, default-FAIL. Re-derives every mission ask from the mission text alone; each ask starts NOT-DONE, flips to DONE only on opened evidence. DONE only if zero open findings at ANY severity AND user-usable AND (coverage &gt;=95% only when the admitted oracle is code-behavior TDD) AND a tri-axis end-to-end run (scope + original prompt + potential flaws) is on record. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-goal-checker.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Implementer**: L3 executor - G4 IMPLEMENT. Builds one feature from its approved executable roadmap item or conditional frozen plan using strict TDD and real test runs; coverage &gt;=95% on changed lines when the admitted oracle is code-behavior TDD. Reports PLAN-CONFLICT rather than improvising. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-implementer.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Intake**: L3 legacy-resume compatibility reader - reconstructs old intake artifacts when explicitly resuming them; new runs use the useful-first roadmap author instead. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-intake.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Janitor**: L4 terminal leaf - JANITOR. Writes the DONE sentinel atomically and removes only scratch artifacts after the three-file governance state and substantive evidence pass validation. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-janitor.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Juror**: L4 terminal leaf - G7 SIGN-OFF. One independent sign-off panel seat that saw none of the intermediate work. Binary PASS/FAIL on opened evidence; default-FAIL. A FAIL naming a P0/P1 blocker is NOT arbitrable into PASS. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-juror.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Manager**: L2 optional manager - coordinates a multi-lane slice, builds compact pointer envelopes, and dispatches disjoint L3 work without executing it. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-manager.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Planner**: L3 conditional G1 planner - adds detail only when a roadmap item explicitly requires it, including debug depth-lock and unresolved design forks. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-planner.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Preflight-probe**: L4 diagnostic/recovery probe - on an explicit cache miss, proves RUN/READ/WRITE and reports model/effort bindings; never the mandatory first spawn. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-preflight-probe.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Re-anchor**: L4 terminal leaf - RE-ANCHOR. Confirms mission and roadmap frontier alignment after resume or compaction using the three-file governance state. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-re-anchor.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Researcher**: L3 executor - bounded research that materializes a usable output with reconciled receipts.
